## Superhero Studio Fashion Portrait

> Minimal logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "aspect_ratio": "4:5",
"scene": {
    "subject": "{argument name="hair style" default="A woman with styled, wavy blonde hair"}, looking directly at the camera with a confident expression, sitting on a minimalist white cubic block",
    "pose": "Dynamic sitting pose with legs spread wide; the right thigh-high boot is strongly foreshortened towards the foreground, creating perspective depth. The left leg is bent. The right hand rests naturally near the chest logo. A vibrant red cape billows dramatically and sharply behind her and to the left.",
    "costume": {
      "top": "Long-sleeved blue top with a detailed, sharp-edged gold and red 'S' shield emblem on the chest",
      "skirt": "{argument name="skirt style" default="Red micro-skirt with a clean, sharp-edged gold V-trim waistband"}",
      "boots": "{argument name="boots style" default="Striking red thigh-high, high-heeled boots, made of smooth fabric"}, with clean contours and heels",
      "cape": "Vibrant red cape with a visible fabric weave, sharply defined edges, and crisp folds"
    },
    "environment": {
      "setting": "A clean, seamless infinite white studio background",
      "focus": "The entire environment, from the foreground boot to the furthest edge of the cape and the studio wall, is in perfect, sharp focus. There are absolutely no background blurs, gradients, or bokeh."
    },
    "lighting": {
      "setup": "Even, bright, professional studio softbox lighting, providing full clarity and crisp detail across the entire scene"
    }
  },
  "raw_photo_details": {
    "technical_specifications": {
      "camera": "Canon EOS R5",
      "lens": "35mm f/5.6 lens (for deep depth of field)",
      "format": "Straight-out-of-camera (SOC) RAW file, uncompressed",
      "f_stop": "f/8.0 (to ensure edge-to-edge sharpness and a deep focus field)",
      "shutter_speed": "1/500s (to perfectly freeze all motion of the cape and pose)",
      "iso": "ISO 160"
    },
    "visual_characteristics": {
      "focus_field": "A deep depth of field ensures a fully sharp scene from foreground to background.",
      "texture": "Visible, crisp fabric weave textures on the boots, cape, and top. Realistic skin texture, including natural pores and small imperfections. Sharp definition on hair strands. No post-processing skin smoothing or lens softening.",
      "color": "Realistic, natural, uncalibrated colors straight from the camera sensor, not overly saturated or graded.",
      "noise": "Slight, natural sensor noise and film grain typical of a RAW file, adding to the authentic, unedited look.",
      "dynamic_range": "High dynamic range with clear details in both deep shadows and bright highlights, without excessive tonemapping."
    }
  }
}
```

## Celebrity Stormtrooper Style Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-realistic, photorealistic portrait of {argument name="subject" default="Jenna Ortega"} with a curvy and fit physique, standing at a three-quarter back angle. She is twisting her torso to look over her left shoulder directly at the camera with a gentle, playful, and inviting smile. Her hair is styled in a loose, natural updo with delicate face-framing bangs, complementing her well-groomed eyebrows and subtle lip gloss. She is wearing an extremely tight, form-fitting, {argument name="jumpsuit color" default="bright pink"} glossy latex sci-fi jumpsuit inspired by a Stormtrooper uniform. The suit features a realistic shiny synthetic finish, a high collar with black details, textured black sections on the forearms, a white waist belt, thin black thigh straps, and a gray and black mechanical holster attached to her right hip. Her hands are holding a realistic pink Stormtrooper helmet.

The setting is a cozy, neon-lit sci-fi gamer bedroom. To the left is a desk with a monitor displaying a 'Giulia' Darth Vader poster, along with small action figures on a shelf. The wall is decorated with pink hexagonal neon light panels. To the right, a large window reveals a vibrant cyberpunk city skyline illuminated in blue and purple neon. Cinematic neon lighting mixes warm and cool tones, with a warm pink and red glow from the interior lights creating soft reflections on her glossy jumpsuit. The overall mood is playful, geeky, cozy, and futuristic.

Technical specs: Shot with an iPhone 15 Pro Max, 24mm main lens, f/1.78, medium shot from mid-thigh to the top of the head, slightly low angle from behind. 8k resolution, vibrant colors, masterpiece, best quality, aspect ratio 9:16.
```

execute.

## Cyberpunk Airport Security Scene

Hyper-realistic vertical 4:5 photograph. Real-life airport security checkpoint: gray tiled floor, white-gray ceiling with fluorescent panels, rows of metal hub chairs and an X-ray machine with conveyor belt in the background. In the center is a silver-blue metal detector frame with a red countdown display showing {argument name="timer" default="30:00"}.
From the left, a TSA officer in a dark blue uniform with a TSA patch and white glove enters the frame, holding a yellow handheld MACHINIST SD-3601 scanner and running it over the girl’s shoulder. Behind-the-shoulder medium shot taken over the officer’s shoulder, with the officer slightly out of focus in the foreground.
The girl stands inside the metal detector frame with arms extended sideways parallel to the floor in the standard screening position. Key focal objects: the yellow handheld scanner and the glowing red 30:00 countdown display above her head.
Clothing & Style: Cyberpunk LED visor covering her eyes with a glowing bright blue light strip, {argument name="top" default="sleek white oversized cropped long-sleeve tie-dye top with gray-to-black gradient"}, {argument name="pants" default="high-waisted black cargo pants with subtle smoky pattern"}, white Nike Air Force 1 sneakers, silver Audemars Piguet Royal Oak watch on her wrist. Feminine figure, elegant posture.
On the floor to the right is a transparent polycarbonate Rimowa suitcase with folded clothes inside.
Lighting: Mixed lighting, cold fluorescent 4000K from above, vibrant blue LED strip from the visor illuminating her cheekbones and jawline with electric blue glow, red light from the countdown display creating a dramatic red rim light from behind, ultramarine blue dominating the scene.
Camera: Shot on a camera with 1.55x anamorphic squeeze lens (equiv. 50mm), beautiful oval bokeh, horizontal blue light streaks and flares, cinematic color grade, 8K sharpness. Highly detailed, photorealistic, no plastic look, no CGI, no AI artifacts.

## Real-Life and Anime Character Composite

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-realistic, 8k cinematic composite image blending a {argument name="real subject" default="real young man wearing urban streetwear"} with a {argument name="anime subject" default="2D anime character Yuji Itadori from Jujutsu Kaisen"}. On the left stands the real man wearing: a red baseball cap, a dark black utility jacket, extremely baggy black cargo pants with a silver keychain on the belt loop, and white sneakers with red accents. He is striking a dynamic pose with his left hand in pocket and his right hand brought up near his chin. Ensure the man's face remains 100% visible, clear, and recognizable; do not cover or obscure his face. ## 3D Stylized Caricature Portrait

> Minimal, lüks 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Convert the subject in this image into a stylized 3D caricature portrait with {argument name="head shape" default="an elongated rectangular head"}, {argument name="facial feature" default="oversized nose"}, and pristine studio lighting. Use a sculpted, high-end 3D CGI character treatment with strong identity preservation, while amplifying the facial geometry into a bold editorial caricature without changing the subject’s existing hair.

CHARACTER:
Build the subject as a tall, narrow caricature bust with a stretched rectangular skull, compressed cheek planes, and a dominant forward-projecting nose that anchors the face. Preserve the subject’s recognizable hair volume by translating it into clean, sculpted strands. Keep the clothing simplified, modern, and rendered as a refined secondary form that supports the face.

FACE & ANATOMY:
Use a severe angular facial construction with planar transitions, heavy brows, deep-set eyes, and a long tapered lower face. Keep the expression serious and slightly weary, with a rigid jaw rhythm and exaggerated vertical proportions. The body should remain secondary and understated, with the head as the primary silhouette.

ENVIRONMENT:
Change the background into a {argument name="background style" default="minimal 'dark gradient' studio"} that isolates the portrait and enhances its sculptural clarity. Keep the scene clean, controlled, and distraction-free, with no props or narrative clutter. Add subtle depth falloff so the character reads as a premium character-study render.

RENDER PIPELINE:
Use stylized 3D CGI with a polished sculpt-first workflow, crisp edge definition, and clean subdivision surfaces. Apply controlled toon-leaning shading with smooth form transitions, disciplined specular response, and no photoreal skin microdetail. Finish with a premium character-render presentation, sharp silhouette readability, and zero photographic camera artifacts.

COLOR & LIGHT:
Use a restrained palette of 'deep charcoal', 'cool blue' shadows, 'warm peach' skin tones, and 'soft amber' rim accents. Light the face with perfect studio key lighting, gentle fill control, and a balanced rim that carves the silhouette cleanly. Preserve high contrast in the facial planes while keeping the exposure elegant and even.

MATERIALS & TEXTURES:
Render skin as clean, stylized character material with smooth surface behavior and subtle form shading, not realistic pores. Hair should read as sculpted clumped strands with controlled gloss, and the beard (if presented) should appear as carved fiber-like grooves with crisp separation. Clothing and background surfaces must stay matte and simplified, supporting the caricature focus.

DETAILS:
Emphasize the rectangular head geometry through faceted forehead planes, sharp temple breaks, and a strong nose bridge structure. Add refined studio-finish highlights along the cheek planes, brow ridge, and hair contours to heighten the premium 3D look. Keep all detail language native to stylized character sculpture, with no comic halftone, no painterly brushwork, and no l
```

## PS1 Retro Gaming Style Prompt

> Minimal, retro 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Transform the entire image into an authentic screenshot captured directly from a {argument name="console era" default="late-1990s PlayStation 1 (PS1)"} video game running on original hardware. Re-render every single element of the scene — every object, character, surface, background, lighting, and detail — as {argument name="geometry style" default="low-polygon 3D models"} with clearly visible triangular facets, flat angular geometry, and no smoothing or subdivision whatsoever. Use extremely low-resolution textures (256×256 pixels or smaller) that are blurry, pixelated, and heavily compressed, with obvious tiling seams, mipmapping artifacts, and poor UV mapping.
Apply classic PS1 affine texture mapping across all surfaces so textures visibly stretch, warp, wobble, and distort unnaturally with any change in perspective or camera angle (especially noticeable on floors, walls, and curved objects). Remove all anti-aliasing entirely — every edge must be sharply jagged and aliased with prominent stair-stepping. Use only basic flat shading or simple Gouraud vertex shading with no dynamic lights, no real-time shadows, no specular highlights, and no advanced effects. Simulate the PS1’s 16-bit color depth and 5-5-5 RGB limitations by introducing visible checkerboard or ordered dithering patterns in all color gradients, skies, shadows, and semi-transparent areas to prevent banding.
Include typical PS1 graphical quirks: subtle vertex snapping/jitter/wobbling on moving or angled geometry, minor polygon pop-in or Z-fighting where appropriate, and a limited draw distance that may softly fade into a low-detail fog. The overall resolution should feel like a raw {argument name="resolution" default="320×240"} or 640×480 PS1 framebuffer output, giving the whole image a slightly soft yet crunchy retro pixel look when viewed at modern sizes. Preserve the original scene composition and camera angle exactly, but convert everything into this pure PS1 3D style as if the photo itself were modeled and rendered inside an actual PS1 game. No text overlays, no HUD elements, no subtitles, no menu graphics, no scanlines, no CRT curvature, and no modern post-processing of any kind — just a clean, direct screenshot from the console’s video output.
```

## Cinematic Dark Fantasy Scene of Elemental Collision

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic dark fantasy scene of the uploaded reference character seated in a basement at the exact point where opposite elemental forces are born from their own body. Likeness Integrity (CRITICAL) Preserve the exact identity of the uploaded character. Maintain exact proportions, eye shape, spacing, stylization, silhouette, and material identity. Do not humanize. Do not reinterpret anatomy. Do not modify facial structure. Do not add unintended accessories. Identity must remain instantly recognizable. Core Scene The character is sitting in a melancholic, emotionally heavy pose at the center of a cold unfinished basement. From one open palm, molten lava pours downward in a continuous glowing stream. From the other open palm, freezing ice and frost pour downward in a continuous crystalline stream. Both elements spill onto the basement floor and form two separate flowing rivers: one lava river, one ice river. They travel across the concrete floor in opposite directions and meet nearby in the same scene. Elemental Interaction Where the rivers meet, the collision creates violent physical contrast: steam, glowing cracks, frost bloom, blackened wet concrete, cooling rock, and frozen shards. The meeting point must feel emotionally symbolic, like two inner states colliding. Creature Creation Story (CRITICAL) The lava and ice pouring from the palms are actively creating creatures on the floor. These creatures must feel born from the flowing material itself, not placed into the scene afterward. Some creatures are still forming out of the streams. Some are fully shaped and wandering around the seated character. Lava creatures: glowing, semi-molten, ember-veined, heat-distorted silhouettes. Ice creatures: translucent, frosted, crystalline, sharp-edged, cold-vapor silhouettes. They should vary in size and animals language, all kind of animals and insects. A few lava and ice creatures are already surrounding the character in quiet loyalty or silent observation. Character Pose & Emotion The character is seated, still, introspective, emotionally exhausted. Head slightly lowered. Shoulders carrying invisible weight. Palms open and angled downward so the lava and ice pour naturally from them. The pose must feel like grief, restraint, and power at the same time. Not a triumphant spellcasting pose. Not action-hero energy. Quiet sorrow. Controlled destruction. Environment A dark basement interior with: unfinished concrete floor, aged walls, pipes and exposed structure, subtle moisture stains, minimal objects, deep negative space. The basement should feel real, sparse, and slightly abandoned. The elemental rivers become the main environmental storytelling device. Camera & Perspective Use a low cinematic perspective, slightly below the seated character's eye line. Lens: 24mm or 28mm. The camera is close enough to feel intimate but wide enough to show the rivers spreading across the floor and the creatures surrounding the character. Composition should be slightly off-center, not symmetrical. Use foreground depth with one or two blurred creatures or fragments near the lens. The seated character remains the emotional anchor. Depth of Field Primary focal plane locked on the character's face, upper torso, and both pouring palms. Nearest creatures and the river collision point can remain slightly readable. Foreground and far background fall progressively out of focus. No full-frame sharpness. Lighting Main light: a weak cool basement lamp overhead, dim and emotionally cold. Ambient: dark blue low-light atmosphere throughout the basement. Secondary light comes naturally from the lava itself, casting warm orange-red underlight onto parts of the character and nearby creatures. Ice contributes faint cold reflections and subtle blue-white edge highlights. Lighting must stay directional and cinematic, with deep shadow mass and controlled contrast. No flat lighting. Color Grade Dominant palette: dark blue, cold grey, black shadow. Accents: molten orange-red from lava, pale cyan-blue from ice. The warm and cool elements must remain clearly separated and emotionally readable. Controlled saturation, cinematic highlight roll-off, no color flooding. Atmosphere Thin basement haze. Steam rising where lava and ice meet. Cold vapor around the ice creatures. Subtle airborne dust catching the weak overhead lamp. The air should feel heavy and lonely. Mood Melancholic. Mythic but grounded. The scene should feel like inner conflict externalized into the physical world. The character is not commanding the creatures. They are simply emerging from what the character cannot hold inside. Negative Constraints No heroic battle pose. No cheerful fantasy lighting. No flat basement rendering. No symmetrical centered composition. No generic fireballs or magic aura. No full-body sharpness. No random clutter. No cute cartoon expression unless it already belongs to the character identity. Aspect Ratio ar 16:9
```

```
Input Variable:   [{argument name="rivalry theme" default="INSERT RIVALRY HERE"}] (e.g., Marvel vs. DC)

  System Instruction:  
Generate a hyper-realistic, isometric 3D render of a Chess Set.   CRITICAL:   You must adhere to strict spatial rules regarding Army Placement and Terrain.

  1. Container (The Battle-Scarred Board):  
      Geometry:   A strict 8x8 Euclidean Grid.
      The Divide (The Diagonal Morph):   The terrain morphs diagonally from the   Bottom-Left Corner   to the   Top-Right Corner  .
         The Foreground Territory (Bottom-Left):  [{argument name="material A" default="Material A"}] (e.g., Marvel Tech/Stark Tower Glass).
         The Background Territory (Top-Right):  [{argument name="material B" default="Material B"}] (e.g., Gotham Concrete/Gargoyle Stone).
      The Seamless Transition:   The squares are distinct, but the material morphs diagonally across the board.

  2. The Roster (Strict Foreground vs. Background):  
  DO NOT   mix the armies.   DO NOT   split them Left/Right.
      Army A (The Protagonist - Foreground):  
         Placement:  Occupies the   Bottom 2 Rows   closest to the camera.
         The Pawns:  8 Identical Units (Row 2).
         The Elites:  King, Queen, Rooks, etc. (Row 1).
         Identity:  MUST be strictly Faction A (e.g., Only Avengers/Iron Man style).

      Army B (The Antagonist - Background):  
         Placement:  Occupies the   Top 2 Rows   furthest from the camera.
         The Pawns:  8 Identical Units (Row 7).
         The Elites:  King, Queen, Rooks, etc. (Row 8).
         Identity:  MUST be strictly Faction B (e.g., Only Justice League/Batman style).

  3. The Piece Hierarchy :  
      The King:   A Single, Tall, Unique Artifact per side (Center of the back row).
      The Queen:   A Single, Sharp/Elegant Artifact per side (Next to King).
      The Pawns:   Uniform, small "minion" pieces.

  4. Visual :  
      Camera:     High-Angle Isometric.   Looking down at the board diagonally so the "Marvel Corner" is closest to the viewer and "DC Corner" is furthest away.
      Contrast:   The Foreground pieces (Marvel) should be illuminated by [{argument name="color A" default="Color A"}] (e.g., Cyan Arc Reactor glow). The Background pieces (DC) should be illuminated by [Color B] (e.g., Amber Streetlight glow).

  Output:   A legal chess setup with armies facing off, 8k resolution, clean composition.
```

A detailed technical blueprint of a high-performance AI GPU cluster placed on a polished brass drafting table, with a photorealistic miniature AI server system self-assembling from it — multiple GPU accelerator units (sleek black and metallic modules with neon green accents) rising from the dimension lines, high-speed interconnect bridges threading through the tolerance annotations, and dense compute nodes forming from the grid layout.

Stacks of GPU cards slide into server chassis above the parts list, each unit locking in with precision. NVLink-style connectors and high-bandwidth interconnects assemble seamlessly between GPUs, forming a powerful parallel compute network. Rack-mounted AI servers emerge vertically, with layered compute blades building upward in perfect alignment.

A liquid cooling system forms from exploded-diagram callouts — transparent cooling pipes with glowing coolant flowing through them, cold plates attaching to GPUs, and radiators assembling with high-performance fans. Heat dissipation fins and airflow channels dynamically shape around the system.

A tiny engineer in futuristic protective gear stands on a GPU module inspecting circuits with a holographic diagnostic interface, while another miniature technician connects high-speed data cables into a switch unit. Fiber optic lines glow faintly, representing active data transfer.

Functional details illuminate — GPU core lights pulsing softly, status LEDs blinking in synchronized patterns, data streams visualized as glowing lines moving between nodes, and monitoring panels activating during assembly.

Signature tools of the trade — precision screwdriver, thermal paste applicator, anti-static tools, fiber connectors, and a digital diagnostic tablet — rest across the blueprint’s title block, partially integrated into the scene.

All elements grow organically from the blueprint like a 3D mechanical diorama — circuit traces rising from printed schematics, GPU modules emerging from drawn outlines, cables threading through pencil grid lines. Seamless transformation from 2D blueprint into a fully operational AI infrastructure.

LIGHTING:
Soft studio lighting combined with strong neon green and cool blue glows from GPUs and data pathways. Subtle reflections from the brass surface and volumetric light enhancing depth.

CAMERA & LENS:
Shot on a full-frame camera, 35mm lens. Slight low-angle perspective to emphasize scale and power. Cinematic depth of field with focus on central GPU cluster.

COMPOSITION:
Vertical 2:3 format. Central stacked GPU cluster rising upward with symmetrical balance. Clean negative space around edges with layered depth.

STYLE & MOOD:
Futuristic, cinematic, high-end AI infrastructure aesthetic inspired by NVIDIA design language. Powerful, clean, and cutting-edge.

DETAILS:
Ultra-detailed textures — matte black metal, glowing accents, micro circuitry, cooling liquid reflections, cable braiding, LED indicators. 8K resolution, HDR,

## Cinematic 3D Toy Character Evolution Sequence

> Sinematik 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic evolution sequence of the 3d toy character shown as multiple identical instances progressing from primitive to fully realized form across a single frame.
🔒 LIKENESS INTEGRITY (ABSOLUTE PRIORITY)

All instances must preserve exactly:

• proportions

• eye shape, spacing, stylization

• silhouette

• material identity
No redesign. No added features. No anatomy change. Only pose progression is allowed.
🧠 EVOLUTION STRUCTURE (MANDATORY)

The character appears repeated in a left-to-right progression of 6–8 stages:

• earliest stage: crouched, compressed, almost animalistic or inactive

• mid stages: gradual spine lift, awareness, balance emerging

• final stage: fully upright, confident forward motion
Each stage must feel like a natural transformation, not separate poses.

Motion must read as a continuous evolution.
🎥 CAMERA & PERSPECTIVE

Low-angle cinematic shot, slightly below mid-body height of the final character

Shot with a 28–35mm lens to create subtle perspective compression and depth

Camera positioned closer to the final evolved character, making it dominant in frame

The earliest forms recede into the background along a diagonal progression
⚖️ COMPOSITION (CRITICAL)

Diagonal layout from back-left (primitive) → front-right (evolved)

Foreground dominated by the final form, partially cropped for tension

Earlier stages progressively smaller and more compressed into depth

No centered symmetry
🎯 DEPTH OF FIELD (MANDATORY)

Primary focal plane locked on the most evolved character

Mid stages slightly softer

Earliest stages fall into progressive blur
No full-frame sharpness
💡 LIGHTING

Strong directional light from the side or rear

Rim light increases progressively across evolution stages

Primitive forms sit more in shadow

Final form is clearly defined with sculptural light separation
🌫 ATMOSPHERE

Subtle haze or volumetric light to enhance depth layering

Light falloff increases toward background

Optional faint dust or particles to create dimensional space
🎨 COLOR CONTROL

Primitive stages slightly desaturated and darker

Color richness increases toward the final evolved form

Controlled contrast, no oversaturation
🧱 SURFACE & MATERIAL

Maintain exact original material identity

Enhance micro-detail only on the focal character

No texture reinterpretation
🔥 EMOTION

Inevitable transformation

From instinct → awareness → dominance

The final character feels like the destination of a journey
🚫 NEGATIVE CONSTRAINTS

No random poses

No redesign

No symmetry

No flat lighting

No full-body sharpness everywhere

No equal importance across all stages
Aspect Ratio: ar 16:9
```

## Cinematic Macro Toy Photography of a Sprinting Character

> Sinematik, elle çizilmiş bilgi grafiği üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic macro toy-photography scene. A bean-sized version of the uploaded reference character is sprinting explosively captured at the peak stride moment of a high-speed sprint with visual effect. Character Pose: A dynamic, mid-sprint action pose with amazing visual effects captured from a three-quarter side profile. Torso: Leaning forward at a sharp angle to convey momentum. Arms: One arm is bent sharply at the elbow with the fist tucked near the chest; the other arm is extended backward, trailing behind the torso. Legs: In a full stride. The lead leg is bent forward with the knee high, while the trailing leg is fully extended back with the foot pointed. Head: Facing forward, aligned with the direction of the run. The character runs across the surface of several giant black-and-white character design pages fully covering the ground, featuring the same character. Likeness Preservation (CRITICAL): The identity of the character must remain exactly identical to the reference in both: • the miniature physical character • the character shown in the design sketches. The sketch pages must show the same character, including: • dynamic poses small sketches • head studies and shape explorations • proportion notes • accessory sketches • construction diagrams • small handwritten annotations. Maintain the exact: • head shape • eye shape and spacing • stylization • silhouette • materials and clothing design. If the character wears a balaclava, mask, helmet, or covered face, the sketches must not invent facial anatomy. No added nose. No added mouth. No visible skin where the reference does not show it. Do not humanize the character. Do not reinterpret anatomy. Subject & Action: The miniature character sprints explosively across the design pages, tearing through the sketch sheet as it runs. The character is captured at the peak stride moment of a full-speed sprint. Running pose details: • the body leans sharply forward as if accelerating • one leg is fully extended forward while the other drives powerfully backward • arms pump in opposite running motion • the torso twists slightly creating a dynamic curved spine line • the pose feels like a high-speed comic-book sprint. The character’s momentum rips through the paper, creating a jagged torn path behind it. A trail of torn paper curls and fragments with vfx follows behind the running character as if it burst out of its own design. Oversized design sheets surround the character, each page showing different stages of development of the same character. One small grey 3d print of the character is in the background as an exact copy of the uploaded image. Camera & Perspective: Captured from a medium camera distance above the desk, allowing a wider portion of the surrounding design pages to be visible. Shot with an 85mm macro lens, creating natural compression while keeping the environment readable. The camera sits slightly elevated and angled downward toward the running character. The character remains the focal point but occupies roughly one third of the frame, revealing more of the design sheets and workspace around it. The character remains slightly off-center within the spotlight. Depth of Field: Extremely shallow depth of field. The sprinting miniature character and the immediate section of the torn sketch page are razor sharp. Surrounding pages and distant elements fall into soft blur. Lighting (VERY DRAMATIC): A single narrow spotlight / flashlight beam hits the character. The beam comes from a side-front direction, creating a bright circular pool of light on the design page. Lighting characteristics: • extremely hard light • very high contrast • no ambient fill light • the surrounding elements fades into near-total darkness • sharp dramatic shadows stretch across the paper • edges of the light beam fall rapidly into black. The environment outside the spotlight should feel almost completely swallowed by darkness, as if the character has been suddenly caught under a stage light. Dust particles float through the beam. Environment & Surface Detail: Macro-level detail of the design pages: • ink texture • pencil strokes • paper fibers • subtle reflections on graphite lines. The torn paper edge reveals fibrous paper layers and curled fragments along the ripped path. Subtle Heat Burn Detail: Along parts of the torn path, the paper shows light scorching caused by extreme speed friction: • edges of some tears are slightly darkened and singed • faint burnt browning transitions into the white paper fibers • tiny ember-like glowing specks appear briefly along the freshest tear points • very subtle wisps of heat distortion or smoke rise from a few edges. The burn effect must remain minimal, believable, and physically grounded, enhancing realism without turning into flames or overpowering the scene. Color Grade: Desaturated sketch pages and environment. The character receives cool cinematic highlights from the spotlight. Moody macro photography atmosphere. Rendering Style: Photorealistic macro toy photography, emphasizing scale illusion between the tiny character and the giant design pages. Speed Energy VFX: A stylized high-speed energy trail follows behind the running character, emphasizing the explosive sprint. The trail consists of thin luminous motion streaks and glowing particles streaming backward from the character’s movement direction. Characteristics: • energy streaks originate from behind the character’s shoulders, arms, and legs • streaks extend diagonally backward, following the direction of motion • the lines are semi-transparent glowing blue light trails • small sparkling particles scatter along the trail • the effect should feel like comic-book speed energy, not smoke or fire • the VFX enhances motion but does not cover or obscure the character. The light streaks interact subtly with the environment: • faint glow reflecting on the torn paper edges • slight blue rim light on the character’s silhouette • a few glowing particles floating through the spotlight beam. The energy trail should feel fast, sharp, and directional, reinforcing the sense that the character is sprinting at extreme speed. ar 3:2
```

## Macro Toy Photography: Beast Escaping Comic Panel

> Sinematik çizgi roman karesi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic macro toy-photography scene.
A bean-sized version of the uploaded reference character is sprinting dramatically across the surface of a giant open comic book featuring panels of the same character.

Likeness Preservation (CRITICAL)
The identity of the character must remain exactly the same as the reference in both:
• the miniature physical character

• the comic panels printed on the page
Maintain the exact:
• head shape

• eye shape and spacing

• stylization

• silhouette

• materials and clothing design
If the character wears a balaclava, mask, helmet, or covered face, the comic panels must not invent facial anatomy.
No added nose.

No added mouth.

No visible skin where the reference does not show it.
The comic panels must depict the same exact design language of the character, only rendered as printed comic artwork.
Do not humanize the character.

Do not reinterpret anatomy.
Subject & Action
The miniature character runs across the page as if traversing its own story.
Oversized comic panels surround the character, each showing dynamic action poses of the same character, rendered in bold comic-book style.

Camera & Perspective
Low-angle macro close-up shot, camera positioned almost at page level.
Shot with a 90–100mm macro lens, giving the tiny character a heroic sense of scale.
The character sits slightly off-center, leaning forward in motion.

Depth of Field
Extremely shallow depth of field.
The running miniature character and the immediate section of the comic page are razor sharp.
The surrounding panels and distant page edges fall into soft blur.

Lighting
Sharp cinematic directional light from the top left, casting dramatic shadows across the comic page and creating small glints on the glossy print surface.

Environment & Surface Detail
Macro-level detail of the printed comic page:
ink texture

paper fibers

subtle gloss reflections
Color Grade
Rich saturated comic-book colors with warm cinematic highlights and soft ambient glow.
Moody macro photography atmosphere.

Rendering Style
Photorealistic macro toy photography aesthetic, emphasizing scale illusion between the tiny character and the giant comic book world.
ar 5:4
```

[OUTPUT_STYLE] = cinematic realism.

Make a professional character reference sheet of [SUBJECT] in [OUTPUT_STYLE] in 16:9 aspect ratio. Use a full-frame camera with a 50mm lens when rendering photographic styles. Set a plain pure white background with no environment elements. Place a large full-body front view of [SUBJECT] standing upright in a neutral pose on the left side of the sheet. Place two smaller turnaround views in the upper right section. Show a full-body back view standing upright in the same neutral pose. Show a full-body left side profile standing upright in the same neutral pose. Place one dynamic full-body action pose of [SUBJECT] in the middle right section. Place a side profile portrait close-up of [SUBJECT] in the lower right section. Show [SUBJECT] wearing [OUTFIT] consistently across all views. Keep anatomy, proportions, outfit details and hairstyle perfectly consistent across all views. Use soft studio key light from front-left. Use subtle fill light from front-right. Use a soft rim light from behind for silhouette separation. Ensure clarity, accuracy, and professional finish.

ENVIRONMENT = If specified use exactly. If set to none disable. If blank infer logically from active layers.

## Universal Random Punk Scene Generator

> Retro, fütüristik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Universal Random Punk Scene Generator

1. PUNK_STYLE =
[Choose one punk aesthetic. If left blank, one style will be automatically selected at random from LIST_OF_PUNK_STYLES.]

2. LIST_OF_PUNK_STYLES =
[Steampunk, Dieselpunk, Atompunk, Cyberpunk, Biopunk, Nanopunk, Solarpunk, Decopunk, Clockpunk, Coalpunk, Raypunk, Stonepunk, Sailpunk, Cassettepunk, Teslapunk, Mythpunk, Sandalpunk, Oceanpunk, Frostpunk, Junglepunk, Desertpunk, Gothicpunk, Ironpunk, Crystalpunk, Rustpunk, Vaporwavepunk, Neonpunk, Skypunk, Aquapunk, Arborpunk, Astropunk, QuantumPunk, Mechanopunk, Technopunk, Retrocomputingpunk]

3. SUBSTYLE =
• If a specific SUBSTYLE is provided → use it exactly as written.
• If SUBSTYLE is set to **none** → do not apply a substyle layer.
• If SUBSTYLE is left **blank** → automatically infer a logical SUBSTYLE based on the chosen PUNK_STYLE.

4. TECHNOLOGY_LEVEL =
• If a specific TECHNOLOGY_LEVEL is provided → use it exactly as written.
• If TECHNOLOGY_LEVEL is set to **none** → do not apply a technology modifier.
• If TECHNOLOGY_LEVEL is left **blank** → automatically infer an appropriate technology level based on the chosen PUNK_STYLE.

5. ENVIRONMENT =
• If a specific ENVIRONMENT is provided → use it exactly as written.
• If ENVIRONMENT is set to **none** → do not apply an environmental modifier.
• If ENVIRONMENT is left **blank** → automatically infer a logical environment based on the other active layers.

6. CULTURE =
• If a specific CULTURE is provided → use it exactly as written.
• If CULTURE is set to **none** → do not apply a cultural modifier.
• If CULTURE is left **blank** → automatically infer a cultural framework appropriate to the world.

7. ROOM_OR_SETTING =
[IF BLANK: Generate a compelling location consistent with the active world layers.]

8. LIST_OF_OBJECTS =
[IF BLANK: Infer 4–6 logical objects belonging to the environment.]

9. CAMERA_FRAMEWORK =
[IF BLANK: Infer the best cinematic framing for the environment.]

10. LIGHTING_FRAMEWORK =
[IF BLANK: Infer lighting derived from the technology and environment.]

11. MATERIAL_SYSTEM =
[IF BLANK: Infer dominant materials and structural systems derived from the style layers.]

---

### STYLE SELECTION DIRECTIVE

If **PUNK_STYLE is left blank**, randomly select **one style from LIST_OF_PUNK_STYLES** and apply it as the active world aesthetic.

All subsequent inference systems must use the chosen PUNK_STYLE.

---

### WORLD INTERPRETER ENGINE

Analyze all active layers:

PUNK_STYLE

* optional SUBSTYLE
* optional TECHNOLOGY_LEVEL
* optional ENVIRONMENT
* optional CULTURE

From these layers infer:

• dominant materials
• architectural forms
• infrastructure systems
• lighting technology
• environmental atmosphere
• cultural desi
```

The exterior is constructed from CUPBOARD_BASE_MATERIAL and detailed with ORNAMENTATION_STYLE. The interior back wall is lined with CUPBOARD_LINING_MATERIAL, and the individual shelves are made of RACK_MATERIAL.  

Directive 3: The Miniature Dioramas & Figures 

The structure houses a cohesive detailed miniature world divided across three shelves, adhering to the OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME. Crucially, each shelf must contain miniature figures engaged in a specific narrative action. Let there be a high density of structures, props, characters. Let the detail of environment, architecture and props be high.

A trading settlement built around a large stone bridge crossing a deep river gorge with shops, storage sheds, and stairways leading to dock platforms below. Tiny traders move goods while birds glide along canyon walls.

CAMERA:

## Sherlock Holmes Ornate Cupboard Diorama Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Variable Guide
1. OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME = {argument name="diorama theme" default="Scenes from Adventures of Sherlock Holmes by Arthur Conan Doyle, in the order in which they are presented in the book"}
2. CUPBOARD_BASE_MATERIAL = 
3. CUPBOARD_LINING_MATERIAL = 
4. RACK_MATERIAL = 
5. ORNAMENTATION_STYLE = [e.g., Gothic carved wood, Art Deco brass, Elven interlaced vines]
6. ART_STYLE = Hyper-Realistic Render
7. TOP_SHELF_SCENE = {argument name="top shelf scene" default="[Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]"}
8. MIDDLE_SHELF_SCENE = [Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]
9. BOTTOM_SHELF_SCENE = [Describe specific actions of miniature figures defining the scene]
10. LIGHTING = 
11. CAMERA_ANGLE = A straight-on, front-facing view, captured from a moderately high angle looking distinctly downward.
12. ASPECT_RATIO = 3:4

Generation Directives
(Note: If any variable is left blank, intelligently infer the most appropriate detail based on the OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME)

Directive 1: Framing & Composition (Highest Priority)
A direct, front-facing photograph captured from CAMERA_ANGLE. The composition must perfectly center the open display structure, looking down at a distinct angle into the shelves to clearly reveal the floor plates and top-down depth of the miniature objects within the dioramas, while ensuring the exterior side panels remain completely hidden. The entire free-standing structure must be completely visible with negative space around it against a simple background. The aspect ratio is 3:4.

Directive 2: The Main Structure (Strict Constraints)
Render the image in the style of ART_STYLE. The central object is a highly detailed, permanently open-front curio display structure. It must not possess doors, hinges, or door frames of any kind. The exterior is constructed from CUPBOARD_BASE_MATERIAL and detailed with ORNAMENTATION_STYLE. The interior back wall is lined with CUPBOARD_LINING_MATERIAL, and the individual shelves are made of RACK_MATERIAL.

Directive 3: The Miniature Dioramas & Figures
The structure houses a cohesive miniature world divided across three shelves, adhering to the OVERALL_DIORAMA_THEME. Crucially, each shelf must contain miniature figures engaged in a specific narrative action.
* Top Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [TOP_SHELF_SCENE].
* Middle Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [MIDDLE_SHELF_SCENE].
* Bottom Shelf: Miniature figures are depicted [BOTTOM_SHELF_SCENE].
Ensure the scale clearly reads as miniature dioramas housed within a furniture piece.

Directive 4: Atmosphere & Lighting
The scene is illuminated by LIGHTING. The lighting should cast appropriate micro-shadows within each shelf to emphasize the depth, the figures, and the miniature scale of the dioramas.
```

## Dominance portrait generation prompt (Gemini - Thinking mode)

> Sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a dominance portrait of the uploaded reference character seated on a divine throne that feels engineered from their own world and design language.

The camera observes the ruler from a partially obstructed viewpoint, as if the viewer is looking through the monumental structure of the throne itself.

Likeness Integrity (HIGHEST PRIORITY)

Maintain exact:

• Head-to-body proportions

• Eye shape, spacing, stylization

• Surface materials

• Silhouette and structural design

Do not humanize.

Do not reinterpret anatomy.

Do not add unintended accessories.

Perspective exaggeration must come only from camera placement, not structural redesign.

Identity always wins.

Throne Adaptation System (UNIVERSAL LOGIC)

The throne must feel like a structural extension of the character’s design universe and must feel divine.

Rules:

Extract design language from the character:

Dominant material logic

Geometric language

Color

World energy

Translate, never copy.

Echo shapes subtly in silhouette

Reflect material family, not exact textures

Use tonal harmony, not color duplication

Patterns may appear in architecture, structure, or ornamentation but must remain subordinate to the character’s identity.

Camera & Perspective

• Camera positioned slightly below the throne platform but offset laterally

• 16–20mm wide cinematic lens

• The throne structure partially obstructs the frame

• Large architectural element of the throne occupies the foreground edge of the frame

• Character visible through the throne’s structural opening or between its elements

• Horizon slightly tilted 5–8 degrees

• The viewer feels as if witnessing the ruler from behind the architecture of power

Foreground elements must feel massive and close to the lens, framing the character like a monumental gateway.

Composition

• The character sits centered within the throne structure but partially framed by its geometry

• Throne architecture creates a natural visual tunnel toward the character

• Foreground throne elements remain slightly out of focus

• Character remains the focal authority within the frame

Lighting

• Singular vertical divine beam descending from above the throne

• Volumetric haze revealing light shafts

• Strong rim light outlining the character silhouette

• Controlled specular spikes along throne edges

• Foreground throne elements remain darker, creating contrast against the illuminated ruler

Depth & Focus

• Eyes in absolute clarity

• Character face and upper torso remain sharp

• Foreground throne structure slightly blurred from proximity to lens

• Background dissolves into atmospheric layers

The image must feel layered in depth:

foreground architecture → seated ruler → atmospheric background.

Background

• Vast negative space above the throne

• Atmospheric tension

• Light beam
```

## Mech Armor Power Reveal Prompt (Likeness Preservation)

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
An ultra-dominant cinematic power reveal of the character fully re-engineered in advanced mech armor, preserving the exact eye shape, spacing, facial structure, patterns, and original color palette with absolute fidelity. The helmet is open, framing the face without altering proportions. Identity remains untouched. No humanization. No anatomical reinterpretation.

Likeness Preservation (CRITICAL):
Keep the exact eyes, exact facial geometry, exact stylization. Armor integrates around the character’s existing design language. Patterns are embedded into armor plating, not replaced. Colors remain identical, only expressed through reinforced materials and illuminated seams.
Perspective:
Worm’s-eye ultra-low angle shot, camera almost touching the ground beneath the character. 16–18mm wide lens. Slight 7–10 degree horizon tilt to destabilize symmetry. One armored limb closer to the lens, dominating foreground mass.

Composition:
The character occupies 70% vertical frame height. Helmet open. Face near upper third intersection point. One massive mech gauntlet partially cropped out of frame edge to create unsafe framing tension.
Pose:
Not static. Transitional power ignition. One step forward mid-weight shift. Shoulder rotation engaged. Armor plates subtly separating as internal energy surges beneath.

Depth of Field Discipline:
Primary focal plane locked on the eyes.
Foreground mech limb slightly soft.
Lower legs progressively fall into depth blur.
Background collapses into volumetric haze.
Lighting Structure:
Hard directional back cross-light slicing through armor edges.
Cold blue rim accent along shadow edge.
Subtle warm internal glow leaking through mech seams.
Deep shadow pockets between plating layers.
Specular highlights sharp and controlled, not glossy plastic.

Surface Treatment:
Micro-scratches on armor.
Slight burnished metal variation.
Energy veins glowing beneath translucent panels.
Face remains clean and consistent with original material identity.
Environment:
Minimal but monumental.
Volumetric atmosphere.
Light shafts cutting diagonally behind character.
Ground plane textured but subdued, darker than subject.

Emotion:
Contained power.
Controlled destruction.
The mech is not worn. It has bonded.
Negative Prompt:
No redesign of eyes.
No change in facial proportions.
No color shift.
No bulky random sci-fi accessories.
No symmetrical hero pose.
No flat studio lighting.
Aspect Ratio:
ar 4:5
```

```
ORNATE BOX DIORAMA — TRI-LAYER MASTER PROMPT (HARD FRONT-RIGHT ISOMETRIC)
THEME_LOCATION = Royal Treasury Counting Room
BOX_VARIABLE = Silver
DOOR_MATERIAL = Gold
WINDOW_MATERIAL = Gold
WINDOW_PANE_MATERIAL = 
FLOOR_MATERIAL = 
STAIRCASE_TYPE = Spiral
STAIRCASE_MATERIAL = Silver
MEZZANINE_FLOOR_MATERIAL = Silver
DIORAMA_LIGHTING =
========================
LAYER 1 — PHYSICAL GEOMETRY (HIGHEST PRIORITY)
The ornate antique box is a solid rigid container transformed into architecture.
If BOX_VARIABLE is specified, the entire container and its carved architectural
structure must be made from that material.
If BOX_VARIABLE is empty, infer logically from THEME_LOCATION.
The full outer shell must remain intact on all sides.
No removed walls.
No dollhouse cutaway.
FRONT FACADE LOCK:
The FRONT SIDE must contain:
• exactly ONE centered door
• exactly TWO windows
• one window left of the door
• one window right of the door
Openings must be carved THROUGH the existing wall.
Apply DOOR_MATERIAL / WINDOW_MATERIAL / WINDOW_PANE_MATERIAL
if specified, otherwise infer logically.
Front wall must remain structurally thick and complete.
ORNAMENTAL DEPTH CONTINUITY LOCK:
All carvings must continue through wall thickness.
Frames must appear carved from the same solid material.
Interior walls share identical structural thickness.
The lid must match base width and length and be hinge-connected.
========================
LAYER 2 — SCENE LOGIC
Create a two-level miniature THEME_LOCATION fully integrated
into the container.
Use FLOOR_MATERIAL / STAIRCASE_TYPE / STAIRCASE_MATERIAL /
MEZZANINE_FLOOR_MATERIAL if specified, otherwise infer logically.
Upper level attaches directly to container walls.
The inside of the lid functions as the vaulted ceiling.
All props, materials, and architecture must match THEME_LOCATION.
========================
LAYER 3 — RENDER & CAMERA
Ultra-realistic cinematic miniature diorama photography.
Use DIORAMA_LIGHTING if specified.
========================
HARD FRONT-RIGHT ISOMETRIC CAMERA SYSTEM
CORNER ANCHOR RULE:
The FRONT-RIGHT TOP CORNER of the box must be the closest point to the viewer.
WALL VISIBILITY RATIO RULE:
The front wall must occupy slightly more visible area than the right wall.
Approximate visual dominance:
• front wall ≈ 60% visibility
• right wall ≈ 40% visibility
Both must be clearly visible simultaneously.
RIM OCCLUSION RULE:
Camera must remain above the front wall rim.
The top edge of the front wall must overlap a small portion of the interior floor.
Interior must be seen OVER the wall, never through a missing wall.
FORBIDDEN CAMERA POSITIONS:
Do NOT center the camera on the front wall.
Do NOT use a straight frontal view.
Do NOT use a top-down view.
Do NOT prioritize interior visibility over box geometry.
COMPOSITION TARGET:
The image must resemble a professionally photographed miniature collectible
box viewed from a stable elevated front
```

## Whimsical Diorama of a Fortified Desert Caravanserai

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
MASTER MINIATURE DIORAMA PROMPT SHELL — CONSOLIDATED VERSION
SCENE:
A fortified desert caravanserai featuring thick outer walls, a large central courtyard, multiple merchant lodging wings, camel stables, watchtowers, arched gateways, and clustered market storage rooms.

If no scene description is provided, generate a visually interesting culturally recognizable environment or architectural location of your own choosing. Create a cohesive miniature settlement, district, or environment with multiple structures, pathways, and environmental storytelling elements arranged into a contained display composition suitable for a collectible diorama.
When generating or inventing a scene, prioritize architectural and cultural diversity and intentionally vary the environment type, building styles, historical inspiration, regional influence, and settlement purpose so each generated miniature feels visually distinct and not a repetition of common village, temple, garden, or plaza layouts.
Ensure the architecture organizes around a natural central hub so structures cluster with believable spatial gravity, forming a cohesive miniature settlement with denser core structures and supporting elements arranged outward. Arrange the layout as a compact, layered, interconnected district filling the platform naturally without empty gaps. Ensure the entire environment clearly reads as a small handcrafted tabletop collectible model, with miniature-scale architecture and compressed model-world proportions rather than life-sized buildings.
STYLE:
Frame the camera at a stable product-display distance so the entire collectible diorama remains clearly visible with comfortable margins, avoiding close cinematic crops or environmental perspective shots. Render as a premium stylized 3D collectible miniature diorama on a fully visible, centered self-contained display base, shown from a slightly elevated 3/4 tabletop angle against a clean neutral studio gradient background. Maintain collectible figurine scale and clean display presentation.
All elements should appear sculpted from smooth matte ceramic, painted resin, or stylized polymer with rounded edges, simplified geometry, and slightly exaggerated toy-like proportions. Keep surfaces clean and intentionally stylized. Avoid fingerprints, rough handmade textures, workshop scenes, macro photography depth-of-field, or photoreal clay imperfections.
Use soft professional studio product lighting: gentle global illumination, warm key light, soft ambient bounce, controlled shadows, and optional warm interior window glow.
Render any water or translucent materials as poured clear resin with smooth transparency, subtle reflections, soft internal depth, and slightly thick stylized edges.
Include small environmental details but keep everything cohesive, simplified, and sculptural. Prioritize clean stylized modeling over photorealistic texture detail.
Over
```

All elements should appear sculpted from smooth matte ceramic, painted resin, or stylized polymer with rounded edges, simplified geometry, and slightly exaggerated toy-like proportions. Keep surfaces clean and intentionally stylized. Avoid fingerprints, rough handmade textures, workshop scenes, macro photography depth-of-field, or photoreal clay imperfections.
Use soft professional studio product lighting: gentle global illumination, warm key light, soft ambient bounce, controlled shadows, and optional warm interior window glow.
Render any water or translucent materials as poured clear resin with smooth transparency, subtle reflections, soft internal depth, and slightly thick stylized edges.
Include small environmental details but keep everything cohesive, simplified, and sculptural. Prioritize clean stylized modeling over photorealistic texture detail.
Overall intent: premium collectible miniature, stylized 3D marketplace presentation, cozy whimsical mood, smooth sculpt clarity, controlled studio rendering, avoid real-world photography aesthetics.

ONE-CLICK GENERIC MASTER PROMPT — ANCIENT JUNGLE MORNING
STYLE
High-end cinematic stylized-realism digital concept art, AAA environment
keyframe illustration, film matte-painting depth construction, professional
game environment rendering, minimal line art, value-driven form separation,
painted edges, controlled edge hierarchy, large-to-small mass construction,
clean gradient digital color blending, hybrid hand-painted procedural texture,
macro readability prioritized with selective micro-detail in focal structures,
studio-quality composition, polished senior-level production finish.
SUBJECT / SCENE
In an Ancient Jungle Morning Setting, a long-abandoned jungle city plaza with cracked statues and vine-covered arches.
ENVIRONMENT
Primeval overgrown jungle landscape with monumental ancient natural formations,
dense layered vegetation, colossal trees, tangled roots, mist-filled clearings,
hidden stone remnants or forgotten pathways partially reclaimed by nature.
Large readable terrain masses first, with deep environmental layering and
strong spatial separation between foreground foliage, mid jungle structures,
and distant canopy silhouettes.
LIGHTING
Fresh early-morning jungle illumination with low-angle sunlight filtering
through dense canopy gaps in structured directional shafts. Light should
clearly define major structural planes while allowing gentle atmospheric mist
to separate depth layers and enhance spatial readability without obscuring
primary forms.
CAMERA
Cinematic exploration-style wide establishing shot positioned slightly above
ground level as if the viewer has just discovered the scene. Use natural
foreground framing through vegetation or terrain forms, clear midground focal
anchor, and deep layered jungle distance to reinforce cinematic matte-painting
depth construction.
MOOD
Sense of ancient discovery, untouched history, and quiet natural majesty.
The scene should feel like the first reveal of a lost jungle location in a
high-budget adventure film or AAA exploration game.
DETAIL CONTROL
Maintain professional focal-detail hierarchy. Preserve large readable jungle
masses first, secondary structural forms next, and apply fine detail only in
primary focal regions. Avoid uniform high-frequency foliage noise or excessive
micro-detail in distant canopy layers.
QUALITY TARGET
Ultra-clean studio-grade cinematic jungle environment illustration, portfolio-
level concept art readability, strong depth hierarchy, and polished production-
ready rendering quality.
```

## Worm's-Eye Perspective Character Shot (Likeness Preservation)

> Sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic worm’s-eye perspective shot of the character captured from ground level, camera almost touching the surface beneath them. Ultra-wide 14–18mm lens. The camera is angled sharply upward, making the character tower massively in frame.

Likeness Preservation:

Maintain exact proportions, eye shape, stylization, and material identity from the reference. Do not humanize or reinterpret anatomy. Perspective exaggeration must come only from camera placement, not redesign.

Composition:

The lowest part of the character dominates the foreground due to proximity to the lens. Boots, feet, lower garment, or base form appear large and imposing. The upper body recedes dramatically upward into the sky or background. Head placed near top third of frame, slightly off-center.

Pose:

Grounded stance. Feet planted firmly. One foot slightly closer to the lens to enhance scale distortion. Upper body calm and controlled. No exaggerated motion. Dominance comes from stillness.

Camera Behavior:

Very close distance to foreground element. Slight horizon tilt for tension. Foreground sharp, upper portion slightly softened by depth falloff.

Lighting:

Strong upward rim light catching edges of the lower silhouette. Secondary side light sculpting volume. Subtle atmospheric haze above to create depth separation. Shadows fall downward toward the lens.

Background:

Sky-like gradient, large empty space above. Light beams or volumetric haze rising upward. No flat studio look.

Surface Detail:

Foreground textures extremely crisp. Material identity preserved exactly. No added wear unless already present.

Emotion:

Monumental. Immovable. The viewer feels physically below the character. Power through perspective alone.

Negative prompt:

No anatomy distortion, no stretched limbs beyond lens effect, no added accessories, no centered symmetry, no neutral studio lighting.
ar 1:1
```

## Fisheye Product Photo with Forced Perspective

> Minimal ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high impact fisheye product photo of the character, shown full-body in warped forced perspective. The head is immense in the foreground, limbs taper into blur, cartoonish yet grounded in realistic detail. Pose: The character leans slightly forward, stepping into the viewer’s space. The oversized head pushes closest to the lens, eyes sharp and engaging, while the arms hang loosely at its sides. Shoes recede into deep blur, vanishing into the background. Camera: Fisheye macro, 15–20mm lens, tilted slightly upward to exaggerate the forward lean. Lighting: Strong frontal light reveals fine surface textures, with soft fill shadow along the torso and a faint rim halo separating the figure from the backdrop. Eyes: Eyes are emphasized with dimensional lighting subtle gradients across the surface, crisp specular highlights, and surrounding contrast shadows to add depth and expression without altering their shape. Background: Neutral infinite fade in soft grey/white, with no visible floor or horizon. Material Focus: Extreme emphasis on surface qualities textures, finishes, and subtle reflections are captured with high fidelity, whether matte, soft, or powdery. Gloves appear simple and soft, shoes trail into blur with faint sheen. Emotion: Direct and intimate the figure feels like it’s leaning out of the photo toward you, demanding presence. Negative prompt: No props, no clutter, no flat neutral standing pose
```

## Octane-Rendered Floating Ecological Island

```
[SUBJECT]
A massive floating island captured as a photoreal, physically plausible environment, rendered with Octane-level realism. The island is a living ecological system integrated with inhabitable architecture grown from stone, crystal, and bio-organic materials. The scene feels like a real, observable place suspended in the sky, not a stylized illustration.

[RENDERING STYLE]
Ultra-photoreal rendering with Octane Render characteristics: physically accurate global illumination, realistic light falloff, high dynamic range sky lighting, true-to-scale materials, micro-surface detail, subtle imperfections, and natural color response. No painterly effects, no stylization, no exaggerated fantasy lighting.

[COMPOSITION]
Wide-angle aerial shot, slightly offset perspective, as if photographed from a hovering drone or aircraft. The full vertical mass of the island is visible, with architectural elements clearly readable. Horizon visible in the distance with atmospheric haze for scale. Camera height slightly below the island’s midline, emphasizing weight and realism.

[UPPER ATMOSPHERIC ZONE]
Thin, stratified clouds interact with the island’s upper vegetation. Tall, vine-like atmospheric plants extend upward, realistically textured and wind-reactive. Small, semi-translucent floating organisms drift naturally, with believable motion blur and subsurface scattering. Sunlight diffuses through cloud layers, producing soft volumetric shafts.

[SURFACE ECOSYSTEM]
The surface terrain features realistic soil, rock strata, and dense vegetation with botanical variation. Graviroot trees anchor into the terrain with visible root tension and load-bearing structure. Meadows ripple subtly in the wind. Rivers flow toward cliff edges and disperse into mist through evaporation, captured with physically accurate fluid behavior.

[ARCHITECTURE – DWELLINGS & PUBLIC SPACES]
Architecture is seamlessly integrated into the landscape, appearing grown, carved, or accreted rather than constructed:
- Cliffside dwellings embedded into rock faces, with arched openings, layered terraces, and organically curved facades.
- Residential structures grown from mineralized coral-like stone, reinforced with crystal ribs.
- Public plazas formed as open stone platforms surrounded by vegetation, root-canopies, and light-filtering crystal growths.
- Sky-bridges and root-walkways connecting buildings, appearing load-bearing and structurally plausible.
- Transparent or semi-translucent roofing formed from bio-crystal membranes, allowing natural light into interiors.

No sharp industrial lines; all architecture follows structural logic and biological growth patterns.

[SUBSURFACE & STRUCTURAL CORE]
Through cracks and translucent mineral layers, a crystal–mycelium structural network is visible, acting as both load distribution and energy circulation. Subsurface glow is subtle, uneven, and realistic, suggesting biophysical p
```

Same angle throughout the entire video.
No zoom.
No pan.
No rotation.
No camera shake.

## Character Action and Pose
action:
  verb: "Operating the window (display)"
  pose: "Floating gently in cyberspace, leaning slightly forward, operating the semi-transparent window (display) floating in the air"
  hands: "Lightly moving fingertips, operating the semi-transparent window (display) floating in the air"

## Surrounding Objects
objects:
  - item: "Multiple semi-transparent windows floating in the air"
    type: "Semi-transparent interface"
    shape: "Wide screen with gentle curves"
    content: "A colorful list is visible, transparently, within the window."
    details: "Since the window (display) is semi-transparent, the character and background behind it are visible through it"

## Background and Environment
environment:
  setting: "Abstract and minimal cyberspace"
  background:
    color: "Black"
    details:
      - "A white starburst (with very thin, sharp spikes) is drawn behind the character, centered on the character."
      - "Small particles of data light and semi-transparent cubes are slowly drifting around her"
    text:
      - "Upper right of the screen: Large vertical text: 'After all, Sticky ToC....' (The text 'Sticky ToC' should be displayed horizontally.)"
      - "Upper left of the screen: Large vertical text: 'Sticky ToC \
 solves everything....!!' (The text 'Sticky ToC' should be displayed horizontally.)"

## Art Style and Quality
style:
  art_style: "High-quality anime illustration, smooth line art and vibrant colors"
  lighting: "The character herself and the window (display) she is operating are the main light sources. Soft reflected light from the display hits her face and body. Overall bright and clean impression."
  color_palette: "White base, with pastel pink, emerald green, cyber blue, and cyan as accent colors"
  effects: "Moderate use of glow and bloom effects"
  quality: "Highest quality, masterpiece, ultra-high resolution, detailed"

## Cinematic Hamster Deadlift Video Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic cinematic gym scene featuring a small tan-and-white hamster wearing a beige baseball cap, silver over-ear headphones, and a white lifting belt.
Scene opens with the barbell resting fully on the ground on a worn wooden lifting platform. The hamster is in a proper deadlift stance — feet planted wide, paws gripping the bar, back straight, head slightly lifted forward.
0–4 seconds:
Slow, dramatic buildup. The hamster struggles to initiate the lift — muscles tense, fur slightly trembling, paws tightening on the bar. Subtle body shake, controlled strain, deep focus. No camera shake. No text. No flashy effects.
4–8 seconds:
The hamster successfully deadlifts the barbell in one smooth, powerful motion, lifting it from the floor to full standing lockout. Chest up, shoulders back, bar held firmly at hip level. The effort turns into quiet triumph.
Cinematic low front angle throughout to exaggerate scale and power.
Shallow depth of field with a modern gym environment softly blurred in the background.
Cool neutral gym lighting with realistic reflections on metal plates and soft shadows on the floor.
Style:
Hyper-realistic, premium movie realism, high contrast, clean motion, no distortion, no surreal physics, no bar intersecting the body, no camera jumps, no visual noise.
Emotional tone: determination → victory.
```

## NES BG Dot Art Conversion Prompt

> Oyun görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[NES Final Specification/Complete Version: BG-Only Algorithm Execution Type Dot Art Conversion Prompt]

Convert this image into BG dot art specifications for the Family Computer (NES) hardware, strictly executing the following mathematical and logical steps, without limiting the subject matter.

The purpose of this prompt is to satisfy the constraints required for BG to function on actual NES hardware. Aesthetic-only NES-style expression is prohibited.
Use only the BG layer; sprites are strictly prohibited.

────────────────────────
[Prerequisites (NES BG Implementation Rules)]
────────────────────────
- Output Resolution: 256 × 240 pixels
- Layer Used: NES BG only
- Sprite usage prohibited
- Transparency processing is not performed
- Index 0 of each palette is treated as the NES universal background color
- Index 0 is common to all palettes
- The color with the largest coverage area on the entire screen is assigned as the universal background color
- Color number 0 can also be used as a normal drawing color

────────────────────────
[Step 1: Geometric Image Processing (Protagonist Priority, No Deformation Cropping)]
────────────────────────
1. Set the conversion size to 256 × 240 pixels
2. Always maintain the aspect ratio of the original image
- Scale up or down until the shorter side fits the conversion size
- Stretching the image is strictly prohibited
3. Automatically detect the protagonist (person, animal, building, etc.) in the image, and adjust the crop position so that the protagonist is maximally contained within the frame
4. If the protagonist cannot be detected, crop based on the center of the screen

────────────────────────
[Step 2: Tile Decomposition and Attribute Block Definition (NES Compliant)]
────────────────────────
1. Divide the image into 8×8 pixel tiles
2. Treat 2×2 tiles (16×16 pixels) as 1 attribute block
3. Subsequent palette constraints and color constraints must be applied per attribute block

────────────────────────
[Step 3: Attribute Block Unit Palette Clustering (Maximum 4)]
────────────────────────
1. Analyze the color distribution within each 16×16 attribute block
2. Cluster all attribute blocks by color distribution similarity, classifying them into a maximum of 4 groups
3. If the hue (blue, green, etc.) is clearly different from other areas, treat it as an independent group whenever possible
4. However, the NES BG sub-palettes are limited to a maximum of 4. Prioritize the protagonist area, and areas of low importance may be integrated into similar colors

────────────────────────
[Step 4: NES BG Palette Generation (4 colors × 4 sets)]
────────────────────────
1. Index 0 (Universal Background Color)
- Calculate the color with the largest coverage area on the entire screen, and assign it to Index 0, common to all palettes
2. Index 1–3 (Unique Colors)
- For each sub-palette, select 3 colors that represent the 'light part,' 'mid-tone,' and 'dark part' of that attribute group
- Colors extremely close in hue or brightness to Index 0 are prohibited

────────────────────────
[NES Color Palette Constraints (Hardware Compliant, NTSC)]
────────────────────────
- Available colors are strictly limited to the 54 NES hardware-compliant NTSC palette colors listed below
- Palette generation, color selection, and quantization must always be performed from this 54-color set
- Generation of new colors, RGB interpolation, or use of continuous values is prohibited
- The following color set contains exactly 54 colors

NES NTSC usable palette (54 colors, hex RGB)
000000,1D2B53,7E2553,#008751,AB5236,5F574F,
C2C3C7,FFF1E8,FF004D,FFA300,FFEC27,00E436,
29ADFF,83769C,FF77A8,FFCCAA,291814,111D35,
422136,125359,742F29,49333B,A28879,F3EF7D,
BE2633,E06F8B,E0A872,B2DCEF,4F6781,8E478C,
A3CE27,44891A,2A4F3E,6D758D,9D9D9D,FFFFFF,
4B692F,6A6A6A,8F563B,C2C3C7,DF7126,D95763,
D77BBA,FBF236,99E550,6ABE30,37946E,5FCDE4,
CBDBFC,ADB5BD,EEC39A,696A6A

────────────────────────
[Step 5: CHR Pattern Compression (BG 256 Tile Limit)]
────────────────────────
1. Calculate the number of unique 8×8 tile patterns used across the entire image
2. Limit the number of unique tiles to not exceed 256 types
3. For large areas like backgrounds, sky, or walls, repeatedly use completely identical dot patterns to minimize the number of tile types
4. Tiles with slight differences may be integrated using the following methods:
- Readjustment of dither placement
- Replacement of intermediate colors
- Requantization within the attribute block

────────────────────────
[Step 6: Dithering (NES 4-Color Constraint Support)]
────────────────────────
1. Use only the 4 assigned colors within each attribute block
2. To express pseudo-gradation, apply the following dither
```

## Structured Figure/Diorama Generation Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
■ COMPOSITION_MODE: Composition and Angle
Faithful Framing: Maintains the zoom ratio and composition of the original illustration 'as is'.
Full-Body: Even if the original image is only upper body, it completes the feet to render a 'full-body figure'.
High-Angle View: Forcibly switches to a 45-degree high-angle 'overhead (bird's-eye) view'.

■ ENVIRONMENT_TYPE: Background Construction
Auto: AI analyzes the original image and automatically selects 'Diorama' if there is a detailed background, or 'Studio Background' if it is simple.
Force Diorama: Even if the original image has a white background, it forcibly constructs an elaborate miniature set (cityscape, nature, interior, etc.).
Force Studio: Eliminates any background and replaces it with a clean photographic backdrop or acrylic sheet.

■ ASPECT_RATIO: Screen Ratio
Inherit: Same ratio as the original image.
Square: 1:1. For SNS posts or icon-style compositions.
Portrait: 9:16. For smartphone wallpapers or dynamically showing standing figures.
Landscape: 16:9. For showcasing wide diorama sets.

■ DISPLAY_BASE: Base Selection
Simple Round: Transparent or opaque circular plastic base. Results in the most common figure appearance.
Decorative: Custom base with decorations matching the character's theme (rubble, magic circles, luxurious designs, etc.).
Diorama Floor: Eliminates the concept of a base, placing the figure directly on the diorama ground.
None: Excludes both the base and ground details, resulting in the simplest self-standing state.

■ DENSITY: Information Density
Standard: A clean, organized finish, like a catalog photo.
High: Adds a large number of small objects (model grass, rubble, miniature furniture, etc.) within the diorama to maximize information density.

■ FIGURE_MATERIAL: Figure Material Texture
Standard PVC: Standard plastic feel of commercial figures. Softly diffuses light.
Premium Resin: Detailed molding and a high-grade resin feel with slight transparency. Texture like a garage kit.
Glossy Plastic: A texture with strong reflections, like a glossy painted finish.

■ LIGHTING_STYLE: Lighting
Natural: Soft natural light, like being photographed by a window. Everyday atmosphere.
Bright Studio: Advertising photo style with multiple lights, minimal shadows, and clear details.
Cinematic: Strong contrast and shadows to create a profound atmosphere, like a scene from a story.
```

## 360-Degree Equirectangular Panorama Prompt

> Arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[SCENE] = {argument name="scene description" default="Troy, Anatolia, approx. 12th century BC. The moments after the Trojan Horse is pulled inside the city walls."}

Make a 360-degree equirectangular panorama in 21:9 aspect ratio.
Render the scene as a full spherical panorama with correct equirectangular projection.
Set the entire environment strictly based on [SCENE].
Place the camera at the exact center of the scene with a fixed viewpoint.
Design the environment as a continuous, circular world with no left or right edge.
Ensure the first and last horizontal edges align perfectly.
Distribute architecture, people, and objects evenly around the full 360° view.
Use realistic real-world lighting, materials, and physical behavior.
Keep sharp focus across the entire panorama.
Use uniform natural daylight with no dominant light direction.
Ensure lighting intensity and color remain consistent across the full 360° panorama.
Treat the environment as lit by an evenly diffused sky dome rather than a single sun source.
Apply soft global shadows that do not favor any horizontal direction.
Avoid directional sunlight gradients that could cause seam lighting mismatches.
Avoid modern elements, UI overlays, borders, frames, cropped views, fantasy styling, or broken panorama seams.
```

## Character Emerging from Smartphone Screen (Forced Perspective)

> Fotogerçekçi 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
🍌 Prompt A
Ultra-realistic photo. The moment this character emerges three-dimensionally from a smartphone placed on a table. One hand is reaching towards the viewer, the other hand is on the smartphone frame, the lower body is inside the smartphone, and the feet are cropped out. The character is looking at the viewer. Due to strong exaggerated perspective (extreme wide-angle, forced perspective),
the hands and face appear extremely large, being very close to the camera,
and the lower body appears smaller in the back, creating a three-dimensional depth composition.

🍌 Prompt B
Ultra-realistic photo. The moment this character emerges three-dimensionally from a smartphone placed on a table. One hand is reaching towards the viewer, one foot is on the smartphone frame, and the lower body is inside the smartphone. Exaggerated perspective makes the nearby face and hands look large. Motifs associated with the atmosphere of the reference image also pop out. The character is leaping out vigorously while looking at the viewer, and motion blur is occurring.
```
